# META
~~~ini
description=Indexed primary arguments retain owned index/argument/result expressions and resolve subsequent member accesses through the result feature, including nested indices, casts, conditional indices and callback scopes (KerML 1.0 8.2.5.8.3, 8.3.4.8.4 and 8.3.4.8.6).
type=file
libraries=standard
require_no_diagnostics=true
require_complete_publication=true
~~~
# SOURCE
~~~sysml
package IndexedAccess {
    private import ScalarValues::*;
    attribute def Vertex { attribute coordinate : Real; }
    attribute def Edge { attribute vertices : Vertex[2]; }
    calc def Coordinate {
        in edges : Edge[0..*];
        in i : Integer;
        return result : Real = edges#(i).vertices#(if i > 1 ? 1 else 2).coordinate;
    }
    calc def Cast {
        in edges : Edge[0..*];
        in i : Integer;
        return result : Vertex[2] = (edges#(i) as Edge).vertices;
    }
    constraint def Positive {
        in edges : Edge[0..*];
        in i : Integer;
        edges->forAll { in i : Edge;
            private attribute selectedCoordinate : Real = i.vertices#(1).coordinate;
            selectedCoordinate > 0.0
        }
    }
}
~~~
